# Third-party notices

The `333` binaries on the releases page are statically linked: the compiled form of
every package listed below is inside them. Those packages are other people's work,
given under licences that ask for something in return. The MIT, BSD and ISC licences
require their notice and permission text to be included in copies of the software.
Apache-2.0 section 4 requires that recipients be given a copy of the licence and that
attribution notices be kept. This file is how that is done. It accompanies every
released binary and every copy of this repository.

333 itself is under Apache-2.0. That licence is in `LICENSE` and nothing here
changes it.

## How this list was made

Each system on the releases page is built twice: the full client, and the small one
built with `--no-default-features --features tor`. This list is the union of every
package compiled into either of them, on every system they are built for, so it
covers whichever binary you have. Dependencies used only to test or to build are
left out, because they are not in the binary. Where the dependency graph does not
say plainly whether a package is compiled, it is kept, so a few entries may name
something that no binary actually contains.

Generated by `cargo run -p n333-notices`, and every push checks that it is current.
Do not edit it by hand.

540 packages, 308 distinct licence texts.

## Licence texts

Each text appears once. Packages shipping byte-identical text share an entry, and
the heading names a text by the first 16 characters of the SHA-256 of its
bytes, because most of these files are called the same thing and say different
things.

### `LICENSE` — `b3d734001a94efff`

Covers:

- ring 0.17.14

```text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

See src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-APACHE and src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-MIT
for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
```
